./DISCLOSURES.md:10:- **Developer Statement**: &ldquo;Yes, BeyondFlow reuses and adapts my earlier MIT-licensed IntentGate prototype components (such as the voice gateway holding pattern and Airwallex sandbox adapter) alongside official ArmorIQ SDK and MCP libraries. All code is disclosed under the MIT License.&rdquo;
./DISCLOSURES.md:11:- Earlier architectural prototypes (such as the IntentGate gateway concept for sub-5-second voice holding patterns and the Airwallex sandbox adapter) were adapted and unified into BeyondFlow under the MIT License.
./DISCLOSURES.md:34:- **Record migration**: the first accepted enforcement run (15–16 Oct) used a Cloudflare-tunnel MCP record named `intentgate-support-mcp` (UUID `cd7deb69-adb3-45f7-977b-f0d0e7235ba2`). Historical captures in `EVIDENCE.md` sections 1–4 are left exactly as recorded.

./flowguard_hackathon_dossier.md:50:   - *Relevance to FlowGuard:* Demonstrates the necessity of automated corridor selection (LOCAL vs. SWIFT) and predictive fee inclusion before disbursing funds.
./flowguard_hackathon_dossier.md:55:   - *Relevance to FlowGuard:* Directly drives FlowGuard's core feature: protecting a configurable Cash Reserve Floor ($15,000 USD default) and dynamically recalculating payment schedules when incoming receivables are delayed.
./flowguard_hackathon_dossier.md:60:   - *Relevance to FlowGuard:* Proves that simple exact-string matching is inadequate; deterministic fuzzy matching and triple-vector proximity checks (Vendor + Amount + ±14 Day Date Window) are essential to prevent double payment.
./flowguard_hackathon_dossier.md:65:   - *Relevance to FlowGuard:* Mandates bank details / IBAN cross-referencing against verified master vendor ledgers before payment dispatch.

./flowguard_hackathon_dossier.md:231:> This is **FlowGuard**: an adaptive treasury controller and accounts payable shield built on the Airwallex Sandbox API, hardened with ArmorIQ intent verification. FlowGuard guarantees that a business never accidentally breaches its cash reserves and never pays a duplicate invoice."
./flowguard_hackathon_dossier.md:237:> Let's switch to the **Payment Planner**. Unlike an unconstrained LLM that might hallucinate payment approvals, FlowGuard uses a deterministic financial rules engine. It calculates corridor fees—including the official flat EUR 12.85 SWIFT fee on cross-border European transfers—and schedules payments based on operational impact. Critical shipments like Apex Logistics customs clearance are marked **PAY NOW**, while lower-priority bills are deferred to protect our cash floor."
./flowguard_hackathon_dossier.md:243:> But FlowGuard's duplicate engine flags it instantly as a **CRITICAL DUPLICATE** using Levenshtein distance and triple-vector proximity matching.

./ARMORIQ_ONBOARDING.md:37:FlowGuard registers one governed agent on the ArmorIQ platform; the same identity is used by the voice gateway and the verification scripts.
./ARMORIQ_ONBOARDING.md:39:### Agent: `flowguard-agent` (FlowGuard Operator Identity)
./ARMORIQ_ONBOARDING.md:41:* **Description:** Represents the FlowGuard operator (`flowguard-operator`) when plans are captured and intent tokens are minted.
./ARMORIQ_ONBOARDING.md:42:* **Attached MCP Server:** `flowguard-treasury-mcp` (`7540cd0a-7d76-414a-a849-96b337a0c6ed`) at `https://flowguard-treasury.vercel.app/mcp`.
./ARMORIQ_ONBOARDING.md:43:* **Governing Policy:** `flowguard-treasury-allowlist` (`23f8f304-0f6b-44a0-9bba-fa9886ca611b`) — allowed tools `issue_refund` + `lookup_order`, default enforcement `block`, priority 100.
./ARMORIQ_ONBOARDING.md:44:* **Tools exposed to the voice agent (via the FlowGuard gateway):**
./ARMORIQ_ONBOARDING.md:45:  - `FlowGuard_orderLookup`: Allowed automatically by the stored read policy.
./ARMORIQ_ONBOARDING.md:46:  - `FlowGuard_payoutChange`: Blocked by the stored policy (`tool_not_in_allowlist`).
./ARMORIQ_ONBOARDING.md:47:  - `FlowGuard_issueRefund`: Held for dual-supervisor approval via a real platform delegation.
./ARMORIQ_ONBOARDING.md:53:FlowGuard implements a full MCP server compliant with the **Model Context Protocol spec (2024-11-05)**.
./ARMORIQ_ONBOARDING.md:56:* **Server Name:** `flowguard-treasury-mcp`

./DX_NOTES.md:18:### Gotcha 2: Tool Name Prefix Normalization (`BeyondFlow_<tool>`)
./DX_NOTES.md:19:- **The Challenge**: To prevent namespace collisions across multiple tool providers in ElevenLabs, tool definitions are presented to LLMs with namespaces or prefixes such as `BeyondFlow_<tool>` (e.g. `BeyondFlow_orderLookup`, `BeyondFlow_payoutChange`, `BeyondFlow_issueRefund`).
./DX_NOTES.md:24:    export const TOOL_PREFIXES = ['BeyondFlow_', 'IntentGate_'];
